| QV-59 | **Bulk Feature creation for IOCC** and **configurable bulk Epics**: the IOCC release table shall offer Select and **Create Features** like DP — the DoD and User Stories of the IOCC bulk Epic's Feature setup (per Release Classification value, DPF-07, DPF-08; no Release / PU sets, DPF-09), the Time Writing story and month Task, all assigned to the QE from the QE Mapping by the release name; a release matching a QMS-0025 Feature 100% cannot be selected (DPF-05) and the others are listed with the reason when skipped. The Epic each stream's bulk creation works under (DP, IOCC, Content) shall be set under Settings → Azure DevOps → Stream mapping → **Bulk creation Epics**: a **shared** default (roles with *Manage shared settings*; saved with the shared settings in Git, audited) and each person's **own** choice (kept in their browser), which wins; built in: DP #13946, Content #22919, IOCC none (Create Features says to set one). The Feature setup of the chosen Epic (personal or shared, CON-63) gives the DoD, release type sets, User Stories and QE Mapping columns. | T | S | UC-02 |
| QV-60 | **My naming patterns** (My settings): a person may set their own release naming patterns (QV-55) for **IOCC, Content and Tool Validations** — never DP or CP, whose naming is locked to the Tool Owner (CON-93) — kept in this browser or desktop app only. Where they set a stream's patterns, those are used for them instead of the shared (or built-in) ones — release names, counts and every figure built on them — and everything is worked out again at once; streams they did not set, and everyone else, are unchanged. **Use the shared patterns** removes theirs. | T | S | UC-08 |
| QV-61 | *(Retired — see DPF-09)* IOCC bulk creation (QV-59) shall give each Feature the Release / PU sets of the IOCC bulk Epic's Feature setup when they are set, else **that Epic's Feature setup itself** — its Feature DoD and User Stories (resolved through the layers, CON-95) for every release type — and DP's built-in sets only when the Epic has no Feature setup. The pop-up shall say which of the three it uses, and the Feature setup editor of the IOCC bulk Epic shall show and edit the sets starting from the Epic's own setup. | T | M | UC-08 |
| QV-62 | Every stream's **Release page** — DP, CP, IOCC, Content, Tool Validations, S&S IT and ECP — shall follow **UI-STANDARDS §10** (approved 2026-11-23), with the same parts in the same order: (1) a **source bar** — view name · source (organization / project, or the Windchill export file or object type) · when fetched or imported, ⓘ Info, **Show: {month} / All months** (one select on every page; not on CP), **↻** and **⋯**, with the warnings about the data below it; (2) the **counts** of the header month as tiles — **Releases in {Month}** (as the Releases table lists them), **Completed** and **Supported** where the stream has a rule for them (QV-58 for CP, DP and IOCC, entered counts marked as such, else — with the reason; QV-64 for Content, Tool Validations, S&S IT and ECP) — at most three tiles, with ⓘ **What was counted** (the counting rules; DP's counted releases stay a folded table "Supported releases counted" under the tiles), and a stream's extra count (CP: QA/QE-signed documents) in the Releases table's count line, not as a fourth tile; (3) the tabs **Releases \| Source data (n)** (QV-47); (4) the **Releases** table with the columns in the standard's order: Select (only where bulk Create Features exists, with "n selected · Select all shown · Clear · Create Features (n)"), **Release name** ▸ (opens the release's documents or evidence), **Release**, the approval or latest document date (Approved; where "Approved" would be wrong the column keeps the stream's own name — CP: Release Notes signed; Windchill: Latest {date column}), **Documents** (right-aligned), the stream's own columns (Type of Release, Container, Completed, Locations, …), **Time Writing Feature** with the match %, **Planned (h)**, **Booked (h)** (right-aligned; on every stream, QV-65), and **QMS-0025 match** last. A stream leaves out what it cannot have (Content: no Completed or Supported tile; Tool Validations, S&S IT and ECP: no Completed tile — QV-64; DP's table keeps its query's columns). The results of the page's actions are Background jobs entries (QV-63). Nothing counted, matched or written by the parts that existed before changes; QV-64 and QV-65 add counts and columns. | T | S | UC-08 |
| QV-63 | On every stream's Release page (QV-62) the **result of an action** shall be an entry of the top-bar **Background jobs** panel (NAV-36, UI-STANDARDS §8), named after the item, not a notice on the page: **Refresh / Fetch from Azure** and **Load from Git** ("Refresh from Azure · DP", "Load from Git · DP"), **Fetch from Windchill**, **Fetch via Windchill tab**, **Load from Git** of an export, **Save locations to Git** and **Load all exports from Git** run as a job that turns into its result; an **import** (a Windchill export, a newer export, results imported or pasted) is a finished entry ("Import Windchill export · S&S IT" — "7 results imported from sit.csv."); a failure (a file that is not an export or cannot be read, a fetch or save that failed) is an entry that needs a look, counted on the top-bar button. The page shall keep only the **warnings about its data**: a refresh that failed while older data is shown ("Not refreshed: … The data shown is from {date}."), data fetched with other columns, a failed save to Git; an import error that blocks the import form stays in the form above its button (and is an entry too). Create Feature and Create Features stay jobs as before. | T | S | UC-08 |
| QV-64 | The **counts** of the Release pages that had none (QV-62 (2)) shall be read from the stream's own data, as Pulse Check counts the stream — never entered or guessed: **Content** — Releases in {Month}: the releases its Releases table lists for the month (each with a document approved in the month, Author Approval Date in UTC, else changed in it), no Completed and no Supported tile (no rule for either: the documents carry only their approval date); **Tool Validations** — Releases in {Month}: the tools the table lists for the month, and **Supported**: the same tools (Pulse Check's Supported line: every tool with a document in the month), no Completed; **S&S IT** and **ECP** (each Windchill export) — Releases in {Month}: the releases (ECP: projects) the table lists for the month (each with a document whose date column is in the month, UTC, read as chosen under Read from), and **Supported**: the same releases (Pulse Check's Supported line for a Windchill stream), no Completed (an export has no completion event); an export without a date column shows — with the reason. ⓘ What was counted names each rule and why a tile is left out. | T | S | UC-08 |
| QV-65 | DP, CP, S&S IT and ECP shall show **Time Writing Feature**, **Planned (h)** and **Booked (h)** in the Releases table (QV-62 (4)), before the QMS-0025 match: the Feature of the row's QMS-0025 match at the match threshold (QV-46, the same match as the column after it) with its match %, and the hours of that Feature's Time Writing Tasks — Original Estimate and Completed Work of its month Tasks and of the Tasks under its Time Writing story, all months, each Task once, removed items not counted — as IOCC, Content and Tool Validations show the hours of their Feature (QV-16: hours stay all-months); "over plan" and the Task count under Booked; — when no Feature matches (a User Story only, or nothing at the threshold). Sorted and exported as numbers. | T | S | UC-08 |

### 5.9 Dashboard (OVR) — UC-09, UC-10

OVR-01 to OVR-08 describe the earlier Overview (Team / Individual switch, "This month" and "Trends" tabs). They are **retired**: the Dashboard rebuild replaced them with OV-11 to OV-14 (§5.9), and the month capacity view moved to Pulse Check → Last month. OVR-09 still applies (Dashboard → Streams). The rows are kept for traceability.

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| OVR-01 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* The Dashboard shall provide, in the page header, a Team/Individual switch labelled "Dashboard for", defaulting to Team. The choice shall be remembered in localStorage `demand-planning-overview-view-v1`. | D | M | UC-09 |
| OVR-02 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* In Individual mode the Dashboard shall use only the chosen person's Tasks, while still applying regional holidays by region. With no person chosen it shall show "Choose whose overview to show." | T | M | UC-09 |
| OVR-03 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* The Dashboard shall show the metrics: utilization (team, or "{person} · utilization"), "Overloaded", "Has capacity" and "Absence". | D | M | UC-09 |
| OVR-04 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* The Dashboard shall provide the tabs "This month · {Month YYYY}" and "Trends", remembering the selection in localStorage `demand-planning-overview-tab-v1`. | D | M | UC-09, UC-10 |
| OVR-05 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* "This month" shall show: "Who has capacity" (person, status, available, planned, booked, utilization, main stream); "Streams of work"; "Hours by Epic"; the "Funded hours" and "Absence" donuts; "Hours by person and stream" (top 8 streams, the rest as Other); and a collapsed "How utilization is calculated". | D | M | UC-09 |
| OVR-06 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* "Trends" shall offer "6 months" or "12 months". It shall show "Hours by month", "Utilization by month", "Utilization by person and month", "Epic mix by month", and Pareto charts for "Overrun hours" and "Ad-hoc hours", each grouped by Feature or Epic. | D | M | UC-10 |
| OVR-07 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* Clicking a month in a Trends chart shall set the selected month. | D | S | UC-10 |
| OVR-08 | *(Retired — see OV-11…OV-14.)* An Epic's colour shall not change when switching between 6 and 12 months. | T | S | UC-10 |
| OVR-09 | Overrun hours shall be Completed Work above Original Estimate on Tasks that have an estimate. Ad-hoc hours shall be Completed Work on Tasks tagged `ad-hoc`. | T | M | UC-10 |

### 5.10 Capacity model (CAP) — UC-09, UC-14

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CAP-01 | Working days of a month shall be its Monday–Friday dates. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-02 | Funded (contractual) hours per person shall be working days × **Working hours per day** (Settings → Capacity & periods → Capacity and absence; default 8). | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-03 | Absence shall be the Completed Work on Month Tasks under the Absence Epic, split into PTO, Sick Leave, Unplanned Leave and public holidays by Feature and Task titles. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-04 | A public holiday booked under a Feature titled "Public Holidays - {India\|Netherlands\|US}" under the Absence Epic shall count as absence for every person mapped to that region, unless the Feature (or an Epic above it) is mapped under CAP-10, which then decides. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-05 | *(Retired — see CAP-38)* Available hours shall be funded − absence − reserved time (common activities, CAP-11). | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-06 | *(Retired — see CAP-38)* Utilization shall be booked hours (Completed Work on Month Tasks outside the Absence Epic) ÷ available × 100. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-07 | Status shall be: "No bookings" when utilization is undefined; "Overloaded" when strictly above (>) the overloaded threshold (default 100%; exactly at it is not overloaded — owner decision 9); "Has capacity" when < the capacity threshold (default 80%); otherwise "Balanced". | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-08 | Streams shall be the Epics above the booked Month Tasks, excluding the Absence Epic. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-09 | People shall be the assignees of Month Tasks, excluding "Unassigned". | T | M | UC-09, UC-14 |
| CAP-10 | Settings → Capacity shall offer "Holidays and common activities": map an Epic or Feature by ID (also one not in the fetched data) as Holidays (absence) or Common activity (reserved time), for Everyone or Everyone in a region (India, Netherlands, US), with an optional name. Each mapping shall list, for the header month, the hours per person, the number of Tasks and of people in scope; name-based holiday Features found automatically shall be listed too. Mappings shall be saved with the capacity settings, audited, kept when the capacity numbers are saved, and shared through the Git planning file (GIT-03) so everyone who loads it gets them. | T | S | UC-14 |
| CAP-11 | Every Month Task under a mapped Epic or Feature shall apply to each person in its scope (people in the month mapped to that region in Settings → Resources, or everyone) instead of to its assignee: Holidays as absence (Public holiday), lowering available capacity; Common activities as reserved time, which counts as utilized hours (CAP-38), not as project work. Hours per Task: Original Estimate, else Completed Work, else one working day. The nearest mapped item above a Task decides. | T | S | UC-09, UC-14 |
| CAP-12 | Region and person views shall keep shared-time Tasks whose scope is everyone or the chosen region; the month capacity view shall show common activities per person, in the Absence metric and as their own part of the funded-hours ring; Dashboard definitions and heatmap tooltips shall name them. | D | S | UC-09 |
| CAP-13 | **NPRA** (non-project related activities: internal meetings such as stand-up, special projects and town hall; trainings such as white belt, AI and Cornerstone) shall be the Time Writing Tasks that carry, themselves or on a Story / Feature / Epic above them, a tag starting with "NPRA" (e.g. "NPRA", "NPRA Hours"; the absence Epic and mapped shared time take precedence). Their hours are not project work: they count in no stream or plan line. They are split into **Trainings** and **Other NPRA** (CAP-38), are not deducted from the available hours and count as utilized hours. They are tracked against an **NPRA maximum** per person per month (Settings → Capacity, default 20 h); hours over it are flagged; 0 tracks without a maximum. The month view shall show an NPRA metric (booked hours, the maximum, how many people are over it), per person "NPRA booked / maximum" with the hours over the maximum flagged, and an NPRA table (booked, planned, maximum, left, status). Unplanned sick leave is not NPRA: it is booked under the absence Epic by the end of the month at the latest and deducted from the available hours (CAP-02); the view says so. | T | M | UC-09, UC-14 |
| CAP-14 | Settings → Resources shall take a **Time tracking started** date (formerly *Joined on*: the date time tracking started for the person, not their real joining date — owner decision 5) and a **Left on** date per person (both days worked; a leaving date before the joining date is dropped), saved with the resource profiles. A person's funded hours in a month shall be the working days (Monday–Friday) of the month within those dates × hours per day; in a month with no such day the person shall have no capacity, not be counted in the head-count or the totals, and the hours booked on their month Tasks shall be listed to be checked. The month capacity view shall name the people counted for part of the month. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-15 | Settings → Resources shall have a **Default Time tracking started** (formerly Default Joined on) (1 May 2026 unless changed; it can be changed or cleared), saved with the capacity settings and kept when the other capacity settings are saved. It shall apply as the Joined on (CAP-14) of everyone without their own date, so months before it give them no capacity. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-16 | The Epic configured as the absence Epic (Settings → Capacity & periods, same ID) is the **Team Holidays and Training** Epic: under it, a month Task that is training (CAP-32) shall count as **NPRA** (Trainings, CAP-38) — utilized hours, not deducted, against the NPRA maximum (CAP-13), not project work and not absence — and everything else under it stays **absence** (PTO, Sick Leave, Unplanned Leave and the holidays mapped as shared time). The Epic's own title is not read for this. Leave and training under the Epic are offered in Bulk actions' Leave and training list (ATM-24, ATM-27), not as missing work. | T | M | UC-05, UC-06 |
| CAP-29 | Settings → People & access shall list the people from Azure DevOps (CAP-51): the members of the Time Writing connection's team and everyone Assigned To fetched work (with Time Writing or, as "no Time Writing yet", without); people with settings who are not in Azure DevOps (historical hours, added by name before v7.0.0) are listed apart under "Not in Azure DevOps", their settings kept (v7.0.0; before, everyone with a profile was in the main list). | T | S | UC-14 |
| CAP-30 | The members of the Time Writing connection's team shall be read from Azure DevOps (read only, names and emails; groups left out) **automatically** when People & access opens and after every refresh of the Time Writing data, and again with **↻ Read the team again**; the PAT needs Project and Team (Read), and a refusal is shown with that hint (v7.0.0; before, an "Add team members from Azure DevOps" button stored them). | T | S | UC-14 |
| CAP-31 | *(Retired — see CAP-51: people are no longer added by name; they come from Azure DevOps.)* Add a person by name shall list anyone not found any other way; people added by name or from the team can be removed from the list again. | T | S | UC-14 |
| CAP-32 | A month Task shall be **training** when it or its User Story has "training" in its title (each person's "<Name> \| Training \| Time Writing" story) or carries a **Training** or **NPRA** tag; the Feature's and Epic's titles and tags under the absence Epic are not read, because leave and training share one Feature per person. A Training tag counts as NPRA anywhere, like an NPRA tag (CAP-13). | T | M | UC-05, UC-09 |
| CAP-33 | Settings → Resources shall offer **Remove** on every person (after confirmation): the person is hidden from the list and left out of **every capacity figure** — capacity, utilization, team totals, Pulse Check, Team overview, My dashboard — their funded and booked hours included, on every page; Pulse Check names them as "Removed in Settings → Resources, not counted". **Removed people (n)** lists them with **Restore**. Saved in the person's profile in Git (`removed: true`), so it applies to everyone. Since v6.0.0 Remove first asks whether the person left the team or is not part of it (CAP-48). | T | M | UC-14 |
| CAP-34 | Settings → Resources shall show **who counts in the month's capacity**: "Capacity in <Month>: n people, X h available = C h contractual − A h absence" (CAP-38), with an Info listing each counted person and their available hours, and a column **Available <month> (h)** ("not counted" for people without a month Time Writing Task, not employed that month or removed); Unassigned Tasks are named as not in the total. The Team overview Utilization tile and its Capacity and workload analysis (Workload by person) shall show the number of people and their total capacity. | T | M | UC-14 |
| CAP-35 | Team overview shall have an **Old Calculation** tab: for one chosen month (a month of the period, the header month by default) one row per person with **Net Capacity** = working days of the month the person is employed × funded hours per day (Settings → Capacity); **Planned Hours** = every planned hour (Original Estimate) — work, absence, holidays, training, NPRA and the shared time that applies to the person; **Planned Utilisation** = Planned ÷ Net Capacity; **Available Hours** = Net Capacity − Planned (negative when planned above capacity); **Status** from the Planned Utilisation in the Settings bands (Overloaded / Balanced / Has capacity / No bookings); **Actual Hours** = every hour recorded (Completed Work), absence, holidays and NPRA included; **Actual Utilisation** = Actual ÷ Net Capacity; and a Total row. Region, role and team member filters apply; removed people (CAP-33) are not counted. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-36 | The Old Calculation (CAP-35) shall deduct **Standard Hours** (32 h by default) from every person's Net Capacity: Net Capacity = working days employed × funded hours per day − Standard Hours, never below 0. The tab shall show the Standard Hours and their **breakup** (lines of label and hours, 0–744 h each, at most 20); roles that manage shared settings can edit the breakup there (Edit Standard Hours: add, change or remove lines; Save), and the total is the sum of the lines. One set applies to everyone and every month and is kept with the capacity settings in the planning data (shared through Git); each change is recorded in the audit log. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-37 | The Old Calculation (CAP-35) shall offer a tick box per shared-time group — **All** (everyone) and **All-<Region>** (All-India, All-Netherlands, All-US): the holidays and common activities shared by everyone or a region (imported history rows for a group and Settings → Capacity rules), each with its hours of the month. A ticked group is counted in every person's Planned and Actual Hours; an unticked group is left out of both. All are ticked by default; roles that manage shared settings change them, kept with the capacity settings in Git with an audit entry. Other pages keep counting shared time as before. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-38 | **Utilization** shall be, per person and month, **Utilized hours ÷ (Contractual hours − Absence hours) × 100**, on every page that shows utilization or capacity (Team overview, Pulse Check, Settings → Resources, My dashboard, Time Writing, Monthly pack): **Contractual hours** = working days employed × hours per day (CAP-02, CAP-14); **Absence hours** = PTO + Sick Leave + Unplanned Leave + Public holidays (incl. shared holidays, CAP-11) + Other absence (Completed Work); available = contractual − absence, never below 0; **Utilized hours** = Work + **Trainings** + **Other NPRA** + **Common activities** — recorded (Completed Work; common activities spread by CAP-11 count their Completed Work) for Utilization, planned (Original Estimate; common activities their CAP-11 hours) for Planned utilization. Trainings are NPRA that is training (CAP-32, or a Training tag on the Task or above it); the rest is Other NPRA. Status (CAP-07) follows this Utilization. The month view shall show per person Contractual hours, Absence hours, Available hours, Project work, Training, Other NPRA, Common activities, Total planned, Total actual and Actual utilization (names per CAP-45); team figures add up the people. The Old Calculation (CAP-35) is unchanged. | T | M | UC-09, UC-14 |
| CAP-39 | Team overview shall show the CAP-38 split: (a) **Capacity and workload → Workload by person** shall list per person over the period Contractual hours, Absence hours, Available hours, Project work, Training, Other NPRA, Common activities, Total planned, Total actual, Planned and Actual utilization and Actual − plan (with the stream filter, only that stream's work counts: no trainings, other NPRA or common activities); (b) a **Utilization** tab shall show, for one chosen month of the period (the header month by default), the same per-person table as Pulse Check → Last month (CAP-38) and the team figure "Σ utilized ÷ Σ available" (and planned), for the people left by the region, role and team member filters. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-40 | In the Old Calculation (CAP-35), an absence Task (PTO, Sick Leave, Unplanned Leave, public holiday or other absence, including a person's own holiday row) with no Original Estimate shall count its Completed Work in **Planned Hours**, so booked leave that was never planned is planned too. Other pages are unchanged. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-41 | **Calculation profiles** (Settings → Capacity & periods → Calculation profiles) shall define how utilization is calculated: for each bucket — Work, Absence, Trainings, Other NPRA, Common activities — *count as utilized*, *deduct from capacity* or *ignore*; for each counted bucket the planned hours from Original Estimate, Original Estimate else Completed Work, or Completed Work (recorded hours are always Completed Work; a deducted bucket comes off with its recorded hours, shared and imported holidays and activities with their Original Estimate when nothing is recorded); the capacity base — working days employed × hours per day, or fixed hours a month pro rata to the days employed — with or without the Standard Hours (CAP-36), never below 0 per person; the shared-time groups (All, All-<Region>) counted; and whether the status comes from the recorded or the planned utilization. Two built-in profiles (editable since v7.2.0, CAP-53): **Current (v4)** (the CAP-38 formula) and **Old Calculation** (CAP-35…37, CAP-40). Custom profiles (at most 20, at least one bucket counted) are added, copied, edited and deleted only by roles with *Manage shared settings*; they are kept with the capacity settings in the planning data in Git, checked when read, and every change is in the audit log. Utilization is blank for a person with nothing booked in a counted bucket. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-42 | The **default calculation profile** (Current (v4) until another is made the default in Settings) shall produce every utilization figure of the app (except a page an Admin views with another profile, CAP-44) — Pulse Check, Team overview (all tabs except Old Calculation, which keeps its own table), My dashboard, Settings → Resources and the Monthly pack — and the profile's formula in words shall be shown with the figures (Info texts, the Team overview scope line and Definitions, the Utilization tab and Pulse Check). | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-43 | **Lock figures**: on Team overview → Utilization an **Admin** shall lock the figures of the chosen month (after a confirmation). The person figures of that month as calculated then — with the profile's name and formula, who locked it and when — are kept with the capacity settings in Git; while the month is locked every page shows those figures (streams stay live), whatever changes later in the Azure DevOps data, the settings or the profiles, with the note "Figures locked on <date> by <name>, profile <name>" (Utilization tab, Pulse Check, the Team overview scope line, Old Calculation). An Admin unlocks a month with a reason; the entry is kept as history (latest five per month) and shown on the tab. Locking and unlocking are audited. The planning and actuals locks (MON-06, MON-07) are unchanged and do not lock figures. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-44 | An **Admin** shall be able to view Team overview and Pulse Check → Last month with another calculation profile (a **Profile** picker beside the filters, listing the default first, then the built-in and custom profiles). The choice applies only to that page and only for that person, is not saved and does not change the default for anyone; while another profile is viewed the page says so ("Viewing with … only on this page and only for you"), and the figures, formula and profile name shown follow it. Locked months (CAP-43) keep their locked figures; Lock figures locks the figures of the profile viewed. Other roles see no picker. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-45 | Utilization figures shall use one set of names on every page (Pulse Check, Team overview, Settings → Resources, the profile editor and the formula texts): **Funded Hours** (formerly Contractual hours, v7.1.0), **Absence**, **Available hours**, **Project work** (hours on stream / Epic Tasks), **Training**, **Other NPRA**, **Common activities**, **Total actual** (Completed Work of the counted buckets), **Total planned** (Original Estimate of the counted buckets), **Actual utilization** (Total actual ÷ Available hours) and **Planned utilization** (Total planned ÷ Available hours). "Actual" always means Completed Work and "planned" Original Estimate. The Old Calculation tab keeps its names (CAP-35). Since v7.1.0 the names come from the calculation profile (CAP-52). | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-46 | **Bucket rules** (Settings → Capacity & periods → Bucket rules) shall set the words and tags that put a month Time Writing Task in a bucket: the title words for **Unplanned Leave**, **Sick Leave** and **PTO** (checked in that order on the Task's title and the titles above it under the absence Epic; other leave is Other absence), the title words that make a Task or User Story under the absence Epic **Training**, the **Training tags** and the **Other NPRA tags** (a tag matches when it starts with the word; on the Task or anything above it). Title words match whole words; letter case is ignored; at most 20 entries of 1–40 letters, digits, spaces or hyphens per rule; an empty word list falls back to the built-in words, a tag list may be empty. The built-in rules are the words used before v5.2.0. A draft shows its effect on the selected month (Absence hours, Training, Other NPRA, Project work for the whole team, saved vs draft) before it is saved. Changed only by roles with *Manage shared settings*; kept with the capacity settings in Git, checked when read, audited. The rules apply to every utilization figure (all profiles); locked months keep their figures; Time Writing's detection of a person's leave and training stories (ATM-27) keeps the built-in words. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-47 | **Insight thresholds** (Settings → Capacity & periods → Insight thresholds) shall set the limits of the Team overview insights, listed once per insight with each threshold's allowed range (the built-in value is shown while editing and wherever a value differs from it): month-end forecast on track within ±10% of the plan; plan accuracy reliable at an error ≤ 15%, under-records / over-runs at a bias of ±20%; stream momentum windows of 3 months, rising / falling at ±20% and at least 8 hours; resilience: a stream rests on one person at 80%, a specialist at 90%; unusual months at ±50% and at least 8 hours with 3 elapsed months of data; plan health green from 75, amber from 50 (amber below green); persistent overload in 3 consecutive elapsed months; concentration: the top 20% of people. The insights, tile colours and the texts that explain them (Info, Definitions) use the values set. Changed only by roles with *Manage shared settings*; kept with the capacity settings in Git, checked when read (out-of-range values take the built-in value), audited; *Use built-in values* restores them. The Overloaded and Has capacity bands stay under Capacity and absence (CAP-07). | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-48 | **Remove** on a person (People & access → People) shall ask whether the person **left the team** — on a date, default today, saved as their Left on: they count up to that day and earlier months stay as they were — or is **not part of the team** — removed from every figure, every month, restorable (CAP-33). When the person has app access, the form offers to remove it too (ticked by default; needs *Manage access*). Nothing changes until Confirm; both are audited. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-49 | **Settings → People & access** shall replace Settings → Resources and Settings → Access (earlier requirements naming either page refer to its sections): **People** — one row per person with their Azure DevOps name, **work email** (the key that links the person to access.json; taken from Azure DevOps, CAP-51), **app access role** (saved to access.json; needs *Manage access* and a Git token that can write), region, team role, reports to, employment dates (the month's figures are on the Team overview and Pulse Check, v7.0.0); **Capacity roles** (CAP-50); **Role rights** (what each app access role sees and may do, and the default role). A person missing a region, team role, reports to, app access role or an email in Azure DevOps shall be flagged "Mapping incomplete: …", and app access granted to an email no one in Azure DevOps has shall be listed (to add them to the team in Azure DevOps or remove the access). Roles without *Manage shared settings* see only Role rights. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-50 | *(Retired — see CAP-57)* **Capacity roles** (People & access → Capacity roles) shall set which **team roles** (QE, QMS, CAPA, Lead, Manager) and **app access roles** (Admin, Product Owner, People Manager, Scrum Master, Team Member; the People manager tick counts as People Manager) count for capacity. Built-in: **Lead, Manager and People Manager are not counted**. A person counts — is Active — when they are employed in the month (Joined on / Left on), not removed and not in a role left out; a person left out by a role has no capacity and none of their hours count in any utilization figure, and Pulse Check and People & access name them with the reason. Changed only by roles with *Manage shared settings*; kept with the capacity settings in Git, checked when read, audited; locked months keep their figures. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-51 | People & access shall take each person's **name and work email from Azure DevOps** — the Time Writing team's members (display name, unique name as email) and the Assigned To of the fetched work (name and email) — shown read-only and never typed in the app; there is no Add a person: a person is added in Azure DevOps (team or assignment) and appears after a refresh. A person Azure DevOps gives no email for is shown "No email in Azure DevOps" (an email saved in their profile before v7.0.0 is used until Azure has one) and cannot be given an app access role here. The app access role and the capacity roles (CAP-50) link a person by this email. The People table holds only setup (name, email, app access role, region, team role, reports to, employment, Remove); with sample data or without a Time Writing connection the team is not read and the Assigned To people are listed, with a note saying so. | T | M | UC-09 |
| CAP-52 | Each **calculation profile** shall define, besides its buckets (CAP-41), what its figures count and what they are called: **Funded Hours** — its capacity base (working days employed × the global **Funded hours per day**, formerly Working hours per day, or a fixed number of hours a month pro rata); **Absence** — the leave kinds it includes (Public holiday, PTO, Sick Leave, Unplanned Leave, Other absence; leave not ticked is ignored: not deducted and not in the Absence figure); **NPRA Hours** — NPRA-tagged hours and/or Training. Built-in profiles include every kind and both parts (figures unchanged). Each profile, built-in ones included, has **names** for Utilization, Funded Hours, Absence, NPRA Hours, Available hours and Project work (Calculation profiles → Names; built-in names otherwise), used by the cards, the Who has capacity and Workload by person columns, the charts, Definitions and the formula text. Pulse Check shows four cards — Utilization, Funded Hours, Absence, NPRA Hours — each with only its formula in brackets from the profile (e.g. [Working days × Funded hrs per day (8h)], [Holiday + PTO], [Tagged NPRA hours]); Overloaded and Has capacity are counted in the Who has capacity table. Saved with the capacity settings in Git and audited. | T | S | UC-09 |
| CAP-53 | Every calculation profile shall be **editable**, the built-in **Current (v4)** and **Old Calculation** included: Edit opens the same editor as for a custom profile (name, buckets, planned hours, capacity base, Standard Hours, Absence kinds, NPRA parts, shared-time groups, status). A changed built-in profile keeps its id and place in the list (so it stays the default and the Old Calculation tab keeps using it), is shown as "Built-in, changed", drives every figure computed with it, and has **Reset to built-in**, which removes the changes. It is saved with the capacity settings in Git (one stored version per built-in), only by roles with *Manage shared settings*, and audited; built-in profiles cannot be deleted; locked months keep their figures. | T | S | UC-09 |
| CAP-54 | The calculation-profile editor shall show the **utilization formula** as *Top ÷ Bottom × 100* and let it be changed by picking terms, without typing: a sentence in the profile's own names (e.g. "Utilization = (Project work + Training + Other NPRA + Common activities) ÷ (Funded Hours − Absence) × 100"); **Top — counts as utilized**: a tick per figure (Work, Absence, Training, Other NPRA, Common activities) with its planned-hours source; **Bottom — Funded Hours minus**: a tick per figure taken off the capacity base, and Standard Hours. A figure is never in both the top and the bottom (ticking it in one removes it from the other); at least one figure is in the top. The profiles table shows the same sentence. It changes only the utilization formula; it is saved, audited and shared as the profile (CAP-41, CAP-53). | T | S | UC-09 |
| CAP-55 | The calculation-profile editor shall offer **Type the formula (fx)**: the utilization formula typed as in Excel in an fx bar — figures in [square brackets] (Project work, Absence, Training, Other NPRA, Common activities, Funded Hours, Standard Hours, Working days, Hours per day; case ignored), numbers, + − * / and ( ), comparisons > < >= <= = <>, and MAX, MIN, ROUND, IF — starting from the ticked formula (CAP-54), with buttons that insert a figure, operator or function at the cursor and a check as you type ("Valid formula · uses …", or the problem: an unknown figure or function, a bracket not closed, a function missing a part, no figure); a profile is not saved while its formula does not read. With a typed formula, **Utilization** uses the recorded hours (Completed Work) and **Planned utilization** the planned source chosen per figure, Absence as deducted from capacity in both, Funded Hours the profile's capacity base; blank when none of its hour figures is booked or it divides by 0; the status follows it. A group's figure (team, Pulse Check cards, Monthly pack, the Utilization section of Team overview) is the formula on the people's figures added up; a locked month keeps its figures and its hours ÷ capacity total; the workload analytics of Team overview (bands, heat map, overload, Capacity vs Actual) stay hours ÷ capacity. The formula is kept with the profile in Git (checked on read: at most 1,000 characters, read by the app's own parser — never run as code — and dropped when it does not read); turning fx off returns to the ticks. | T | S | UC-09 |
| CAP-56 | A person's own capacity and hours (Time Writing tiles, My dashboard) shall be found under **every name they go by**: the chosen or derived name and each Assigned To display name of items carrying their work email (letter case ignored). When they are not in the month's capacity, the Capacity tile on Time Writing and My hours on My dashboard (v7.22.0) shall say why instead of showing 0h: marked removed, a role not counted for capacity, not employed on a working day of the month (Joined on / Left on), or no month Time Writing Task yet. | T | M | UC-04 |
| CAP-57 | **Capacity roles** (People & access → Capacity roles) shall set which **team roles** (QE, QMS, CAPA, Lead, Manager) count for capacity; only a person's **team Role** decides it — never their **app access role** (Admin, Product Owner, People Manager, Scrum Master, Team Member) nor the **People manager** tick, which only grant rights in the app. Built-in: **Lead and Manager are not counted**. A person counts when employed in the month (Joined on / Left on), not removed and not in a team role left out; a person left out has no capacity and none of their hours count in any utilization figure, and Pulse Check and People & access name them with the reason. Changed only by roles with *Manage shared settings*; kept with the capacity settings in Git, checked when read, audited; locked months keep their figures. | T | M | UC-09 |

### 5.11 Baselines (BAS) — UC-11

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| BAS-01 | A release shall be a Feature, not Removed, whose parent Epic title equals a rule's stream (trimmed, case-insensitive). | T | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-02 | Every stream shall use the same formula: baseline hours = hours per unit × (fixed units per release + units per driver × driver count). When a maximum is set and the result exceeds it, the baseline shall equal the maximum. There shall be no release types. | T | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-03 | Actual hours for a release shall be the Completed Work (negatives treated as 0) on its Month Tasks, and on Tasks under its Time Writing stories, rolled up to the nearest Feature. | T | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-04 | The table shall show, per release: stream, release, scope, baseline, actual, variance (actual − current baseline), and scope growth (current baseline − first baseline). | T | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-05 | "Set scope" / "Re-baseline" shall require the driver count (when the rule has units per driver > 0) before "Save baseline" is enabled. | T | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-06 | Saving a scope shall append a version {time, driver count, reason} to the release's history and write an audit entry "Baseline" or "Re-baseline". | T | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-07 | When a scope is identical to the current one, the form shall warn "Same scope as the current baseline; saving adds a version with no change." and still allow saving. | D | C | UC-11 |
| BAS-08 | The rules editor shall maintain one row per stream with: hours per unit, fixed units per release, driver, units per driver and maximum hours, and shall show the resulting formula. It shall warn about streams whose hours per unit is 0. | D | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-09 | Saving rules shall drop empty or duplicate stream names, replace negative or non-finite numbers with 0, and drop a maximum of 0. Rules saved in the earlier shape (release types and line items) shall be read as the uniform formula: the first line item is kept, and a types-only rule becomes 0h per document. Saving shall write an audit "Update"/"BaselineRules". | T | M | UC-11 |
| BAS-10 | "Load example values" shall load the example rules into the draft without saving. | D | C | UC-11 |
| BAS-11 | The "Task plan check · this month" panel shall flag a Month Task with ">25%" when Original Estimate > 0 and Completed Work > 1.25 × Original Estimate. See OI-07. | T | S | UC-11 |

### 5.12 Git storage (GIT) — UC-12

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GIT-01 | The app shall accept an owner and repository matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,100}$` (a trailing `.git` is removed), a branch matching `^[A-Za-z0-9._/-]{1,200}$` without `..`, and a path ending in `.json` without empty or `..` segments. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-02 | The app shall keep the GitHub token in localStorage `demand-planning-git-token-v1` only when "Remember token in this browser" is ticked, and otherwise in memory for the session. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-03 | The Git file shall contain exactly `{schema: 1, baselineRules, releaseScopes, resourceProfiles, capacitySettings}`, as 2-space-indented JSON with a trailing newline; `capacitySettings` (absence Epic, hours per day, thresholds, holidays and common activities sorted by ID) is written once the app has capacity settings, and a file without it stays valid. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-04 | "Connect & load" and "Load latest" shall replace those fields in the app with the file's content (the capacity settings only when the file has them). When the file does not exist and the branch exists, the app shall report that saving will create it. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-05 | The app shall reject a file that is not JSON, or is not schema 1, loading nothing. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-06 | "Save to Git" shall write the file with the loaded version id (sha) and the entered change description as the commit message. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-07 | When GitHub reports a conflict (HTTP 409 or 422), the app shall show "The data file changed in Git since it was loaded here. Load the latest version, then make your change again." and set the status "Conflict". | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-08 | The sync bar shall show "Unsaved changes — not yet in Git" whenever the current fields differ from the last synced file; it shall also be shown on Settings → Capacity. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-09 | In sample mode the app shall refuse to save to Git. | T | M | UC-12, UC-18 |
| GIT-10 | "Disconnect" shall remove the Git config and token from this browser, leaving app data and the repository unchanged. | T | S | UC-12 |
| GIT-11 | The app shall map HTTP 401, 403 and 404 to the specific messages listed in the code (`gitStorage.ts`). | T | S | UC-12 |
| GIT-12 | When Git is connected and a token is available, the app shall load the Git file automatically once at start, and check it again while the app is open (GIT-15). | D | S | UC-12 |
| GIT-13 | The Connect Git storage form shall start with the team's data repository prefilled — owner `ponkritz`, repository `DemandPlanningStorage`, branch `main`, data file `planning-data.json` — so that a user adds only the token; the values stay editable, and a saved connection shows its own values instead. The Get started guide shall say so. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-14 | The shared planning file (planning-data.json) shall also hold **Settings → Table defaults** (UI-12) and the **release-source settings** (fuzzy match threshold and approval date field of Stream mapping), so that Save to Git shares them with everyone: each written once set, checked when read (unknown values and an invalid threshold dropped), and an older file without them leaves the browser's own values alone. A changed match threshold shows the Git save bar on Stream mapping. Before v7.7.0 both stayed in the browser where they were changed. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-15 | While the app is open it shall pick up what others save to Git: every **5 minutes**, and when the window comes back (at most once a minute), it lists the folders of the shared files it has read or saved (planning data, Access, Feature setup, QE Mapping, the On-Demand pages, the connection setup, the Monthly pack setup and month text, default and own views, historical hours) — a folder listing, no file contents — and compares their versions with the ones it has. **Newer planning data** is loaded at once (quietly, without reloading other areas) unless this browser has unsaved planning changes; the Storage message says "Updated from Git at …". Every other newer file, and planning data with unsaved changes, is named in a **"Newer data in Git" banner** with **Reload** (which says when reloading drops unsaved planning changes). Files saved from this browser do not count; no check with the synthetic sample or without Git storage. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-16 | Loading the planning data from Git (**Load latest**, **Update & load**, **Load latest from Git** on an error or a save conflict, and Data & sharing → Load everything) shall first ask for confirmation when this browser has changes not yet saved to Git, naming what is replaced (baselines, release scope, the region / role mapping, the capacity settings, table defaults) and, on a conflict, that someone else saved first; declined, nothing is loaded and the changes stay. Without unsaved changes it loads without asking. | T | M | UC-12 |
| GIT-17 | Saves to the shared Git files that are merged rather than refused — historical hours (HIS-04), default views (MYD-09), the KPI tracker copy (KPT-03), connection backups (CON-88), the On-Demand pages (OND-09) and the Monthly pack template (PCK-08) — shall be **queued with their time (to the second)** and written one after another in that order, each as **its own commit** whose message ends with `[saved <UTC time>]`. Before each write the file shall be read again from Git; when someone else changed it after it was read here, the change shall be **merged** into the latest content by the file's entries (rows, pages, sheets, connections), keeping both changes and, for an entry both changed, the one with the later time (from the other commit's `[saved …]`, else its commit date); a change wins over a removal (for the On-Demand pages the later time wins there too, OND-09). The template is binary and not merged: the latest time wins, and an older save is not written. A conflict at write time shall read, merge and try again, at most **3 times**; then the save shall fail with "… kept changing in Git while saving (3 tries). Your change is kept here and not lost: choose Retry to save it again." and stay in the queue until **Retry** (or the page is closed). Where the save happens the app shall show **"Waiting to save (N in queue)"** while more than one save of the file waits, **"Saved to Git — merged with a change by someone else"** when merged, and the kept changes with Retry. Access, Epic / Feature setup, QE Mapping, the Monthly pack setup and month text and the planning data keep refusing a conflict (GIT-07). (Design defaults review, decision 8.) | T | M | UC-12 |

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| SNP-02 | *(Retired — moved to the backlog: SharePoint is not functional; see PROJECT-PLAN.md → Backlog)* When connected, the app shall save the full app state to the central SharePoint file 900 ms after the last change, using the file's etag. | T | M | UC-13 |
| SNP-03 | *(Retired — moved to the backlog: SharePoint is not functional; see PROJECT-PLAN.md → Backlog)* When SharePoint rejects a save because the etag changed (HTTP 412), the app shall show "Another user updated the central data. Pull the latest version before editing again." | T | M | UC-13 |
| SNP-04 | The snapshot buttons "Save snapshot to Git" (LTE) and "Save PI snapshot to Git" shall be disabled unless Git storage is connected with a token that can write, the synthetic sample is not loaded and no save is running; the page names the destination (repository and folder) and warns before it is closed while a save runs. | T | M | UC-13 |
| SNP-05 | A snapshot shall contain its format, version, id, title, type, month, creation time, creator, build, and **all the data present at the moment** (owner decision 11; format version 2): schemaVersion, people, activities, efforts, baselines, periods, releases, audits, masters, the work items with their hours (azureWorkItems), azureBacklogScope, setupLinks, capacitySettings, baselineRules, releaseScopes, resourceProfiles, the release data (releaseSource, releasePlans, windchillSources, azureSources), tableDefaults and releaseSourceSettings — never earlier snapshots, the data of My views or anything outside the app data. | T | M | UC-13 |
| SNP-06 | A snapshot shall be saved to the Git data repository as "{planning data folder}/lte-snapshots/{month}-{type}-{id}.json", one commit per snapshot, as a **new file only** (an existing file is never replaced). It shall be read back and compared byte for byte before success is reported. | T | M | UC-13 |
| SNP-07 | The app shall reject a snapshot larger than 50 MB, and shall never store a snapshot in the browser. | T | M | UC-13 |
| SNP-08 | Snapshot history shall list only matching snapshot files of the lte-snapshots folder in Git, newest month first, each with a link to the file in GitHub, its month and type. | T | M | UC-13 |
| SNP-09 | The Snapshots page shall offer CSV and JSON exports. | D | S | UC-15 |

The menu group **Admin** shall hold the shared setup, one page per area, each shown only to people allowed at least one of its permissions (CON-94): **Overview** (ADM-01), **Streams** (the stream mapping, CON-93), **Stream options** (Epics to match Features in, Bulk creation Epics, Release counts), **Epic & Feature setup** (EPS-08), **People & roles** (People, Capacity roles, Role rights), **Capacity & time** (Capacity and absence, Calculation profiles, Bucket rules, Insight thresholds, Holidays and common activities, Period control, Historical hours), **Reports** (Monthly pack, Table and chart defaults), **Data & sharing** (Git storage, Load from Git, Shared setup in Git, Other views, Read-only sources, Synthetic sample), **History** (the audit). The header shall name the page. A page with several parts shall show them as sections, one at a time, without a row when there is only one; the last page and section shall be remembered in this browser. Links and remembered choices to the former pages and sections (Azure DevOps → Servers and PATs, Your work email, Other views…; Access; Table defaults; Historical hours) shall open where they moved. | T | S | UC-01, UC-14 |
| SET-16 | Every settings section shall show a **scope chip** saying whom a change affects, before it is made: **Only you · This computer** (My settings), **This computer** (Git storage, Load from Git, Read-only sources, Synthetic sample), **Everyone · Shared in Git** (shared setup), **Yours or everyone's** (sections with a personal and a shared layer: Feature setup, Bulk creation Epics, Other views) and **Everyone · Tool Owner only** (Streams, Shared setup in Git); its tooltip explains it. | I | S | UC-14 |
| SET-17 | **My views** (My settings) shall let a person add their own **read-only** views: a name, a server they already have a PAT for (the server's PAT is used; on-premises servers stay read-only), a project and a WIQL query. A view shall be listed under **Other views** in that person's menu and fetched like other views, and shall be kept in this browser or desktop app only: never in the shared setup in Git, its data never saved to Git or a JSON backup, kept when an admin resets the shared connection setup, and not listed in Data & sharing → Other views. A view shall never replace or change a stream: a name that names a stream (DP, CP, IOCC, Content, Tool Validations…) is refused, and a view is never matched to a stream or data set. A duplicate of another view (same server, project and query) is refused. | T | S | UC-14 |
| SET-18 | The settings of how the app looks shall be on **one page, Look & feel**, opened from the profile menu, with a tab each: **Page layout** (NAV-50), **Theme and zoom** (Follow the computer / Light / Dark, in step with the top-bar theme button; the desktop zoom stays in the footer), **Start view and menu** (Tiles or Classic, NAV-46; Customize menu) — these three only for the person on this computer — and **Table and chart defaults** (UI-12, UI-17; shared with everyone, only for roles allowed "Table, chart and page defaults"), which moves here from Admin → Reports; an older link to it opens here. | T | S | UC-01, UC-14 |

### 5.15 Local storage, backup and recovery (STO) — UC-15

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| STO-01 | The app shall save the app state to IndexedDB 200 ms after the last change, with saves queued in order. A save shall count as done only when its transaction completes. | T | M | UC-15 |
| STO-02 | On start, the app shall load IndexedDB. Otherwise it shall migrate the legacy localStorage copy `demand-planning-mvp-v1` (keeping it), and otherwise it shall use the seed state. | T | M | UC-15 |
| STO-03 | When saved data cannot be read, the app shall not fall back to the seed state and shall not overwrite the saved data. It shall show "Saved data could not be loaded. Nothing was overwritten." and a "Retry loading saved data" button. | T | M | UC-15 |
| STO-04 | When a save fails, the app shall show a global error with "Export JSON backup now" and "Retry browser save". | T | M | UC-15 |
| STO-05 | While a local save or a snapshot save is pending, the app shall warn before the page is unloaded. | D | S | UC-15 |
| STO-06 | "Export current JSON backup" shall download the full app state as `demand-planning-backup-YYYY-MM-DD.json`. | T | M | UC-15 |
| STO-07 | Restore shall reject: a file over 200 MB; a file that is not an object; schemaVersion ≠ 1; required arrays missing; optional fields of the wrong shape; and Azure work items missing required fields. In each case the existing data shall be left unchanged. | T | M | UC-15 |
| STO-08 | Restore shall require the confirmation checkbox. | T | M | UC-15 |
| STO-09 | PATs, connection profiles and the Git token shall never be part of the app state, a JSON backup, a snapshot or a Git file. | I, T | M | UC-15 |

| ID | Requirement | Ver. | Pri. | Trace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UPD-01 | The build shall emit `build-info.json` with app, formatVersion, version, buildId, builtAt, revision and buildNumber, and inject `<meta name="app-build-id">` into the HTML. | T, I | M | UC-16, UC-19 |
| UPD-02 | "↻ Force Update" shall open a dialog showing the running version, build number, build ID, built time and revision, and the deployed build. | D | M | UC-16 |
| UPD-03 | The reload shall be blocked, with the first applicable reason shown, while: a fetch is running; a save is pending; a failed save or an unsaved preview exists without a JSON export; or the acknowledgement is not ticked. | T | M | UC-16 |
| UPD-04 | The app shall reload only when the fetched HTML's build id equals the deployed manifest's build id. Otherwise it shall show "The deployment is still propagating, or the server returned an older page. Please retry shortly. Nothing was reloaded." | T | M | UC-16 |
| UPD-05 | The update check shall use no-store, no credentials, no redirects and a 15 s timeout. | T | M | UC-16 |
| UPD-06 | Force Update shall not clear IndexedDB data, PAT settings or column preferences. | T | M | UC-16 |

| DSK-02 | The desktop window shall run with contextIsolation on, nodeIntegration off and sandbox on. It shall deny new windows, opening https links in the system browser. | I | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-03 | The desktop app shall accept IPC requests only from its own main frame (the bundled index.html, or the dev server origin). | T | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-04 | The desktop Azure DevOps proxy shall allow only the read operations areas, backlog-epics, iterations, iteration-tree, fields, query, items, team-members, comments and updates (one work item's history, FET-24), plus the constrained writes create-feature, create-child, update-time-writing-effort and update-work-item. | T | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-05 | The desktop proxy shall try API versions 7.1, 6.0 and 5.0 on a version error, and shall remember the working one per collection. | T | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-06 | The desktop proxy shall not follow redirects. It shall report a redirect as a sign-in problem. | T | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-07 | "Check for updates" shall read `latest.json` from `https://github.com/neurolooom-eng/Test2/releases/download/desktop-latest/` and report an update only when its buildNumber is greater than the installed one. | T | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-08 | The desktop updater shall download only the installer named for this OS from that fixed address. It shall install it only when its size and SHA-256 equal `latest.json`, and shall delete it otherwise. | T | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-09 | On Windows the updater shall start the verified installer and close the app. On macOS it shall open the verified disk image. | D | M | UC-17 |
| DSK-10 | The desktop app shall check quietly 5 s after start and every 4 hours. It shall show "Update available" with "Update now" and "Later". "Later" shall hide the notice until a newer build appears. | D | S | UC-17 |

| DOC-04 | A link to another bundled document shall open that document in the page, at the linked section when an anchor is given. A link to a section shall scroll to it. | T, D | M | UC-20 |
| DOC-05 | Only https links shall be clickable external links, opening in a new tab. Links of any other scheme shall be shown as plain text. | T | M | UC-20 |
| DOC-06 | The page shall show an "On this page" list of the document's level-2 sections, linking to each. | D | S | UC-20 |
| DOC-07 | "Download .md" shall download the shown document under its file name. | D | S | UC-20 |
| DOC-08 | The page shall remember the selected document in localStorage `demand-planning-docs-tab-v1`. | D | C | UC-20 |
| DOC-09 | The repository shall hold a **Validation Package** indexed by `docs/VALIDATION-PACKAGE.md`: the requirements at three levels (user, system, detailed), the generated traceability matrix, the test plan, the verification and UAT plans, the known-issues register (known bugs, open issues, accepted residual risks and limitations), the software components (in-house, third-party with versions and licences, platforms, known vulnerabilities), the security assessment (code analysis, penetration test, product security risk assessment, mitigations traced to requirements and test cases), the DFMEA (controls traced to requirements) and the development process mapped to ISO 13485 §7.3 / §4.1.6, IEC 62304 and the FDA CSA guidance. Every package document is bundled into the build and shown on the Documentation page. | I | M | UC-20 |
| DOC-10 | `node scripts/traceability.mjs` shall generate `docs/TRACEABILITY-MATRIX.md` from the documents — use case → user requirement → system requirement → detailed requirement → test case → UAT day → automated test → software component, with a gap list — and `pnpm test` shall fail when the committed matrix differs from the generated one, a requirement in force has no test case, a test case names an unknown requirement, a UAT day names an unknown or retired test case, a use case has no user requirement, a user requirement no system requirement, a system requirement no detailed requirement, an ID is defined twice, or a source file is not listed as a software component. | T | M | UC-19, UC-20 |
| DOC-11 | `pnpm test` shall check the Validation Package documents: each has its required sections; finding, risk, mitigation, DFMEA and issue IDs are unique; every requirement, test case, mitigation, DFMEA row, finding, risk or issue ID cited in a package document exists in the document that defines it; the package index lists every package document and the app bundles them. | T | M | UC-20 |
| DOC-12 | In Web and Windows Desktop, Documentation shall offer a Tool Validation Package area listing and downloading supported files only from `tool-validation/demandplanning` and its defined category folders in the configured Git storage repository and branch. It shall use the configured PAT in authenticated GET headers; Contents: Read shall suffice. It shall display category, original format and draft status, provide search and explicit refresh, handle missing or denied access, and preserve binary bytes above 1 MB. Neither private files nor the PAT shall be bundled or persisted in app data/exports; PATs shall never occur in download URLs or errors. Blank templates shall be distinguished from draft records; downloads shall not imply approval or execution. | T, I | M | UC-20 |
| DOC-13 | The Documentation page shall offer a Design documents area that bundles every Markdown file under `docs/design/`, including nested folders, with the design index as its default entry. Users shall be able to navigate between bundled documents, open repository source links on GitHub and download the displayed document as Markdown. The area shall use the app’s existing Documentation access. | T, D | M | UC-20 |
